([Kasempiternal/Claude-Agent-System](https://github.com/Kasempiternal/Claude-Agent-System/blob/HEAD/claude-agent-system-plugin/agents/review-guidelines.md) · ★15)
- **review-guidelines**: Guidelines reviewer for code review. Checks compliance with project rules, conventions, and established patterns. Requires diff output, diff stat output, and PR/MR description (if any) as context. ([ttakanawa/dotfiles](https://github.com/ttakanawa/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-guidelines.md))
- **review-guides**: Reviews a PR for conformance to CLAUDE.md and docs/engineering/*. Use as part of /code-review orchestration. Flags violations of project conventions, idioms, DI rules, layering, comment style, commit naming. Does not check correctness or platform parity. ([khmelevartem/tether](https://github.com/khmelevartem/tether/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-guides.md) · ★1)
- **review-helper**: Read-only subagent of warden: gathers evidence for one bounded question about a pull request, such as one file, one claim or one repro, and reports facts without judging the verdict. ([DresvyanskiyDenis/claude-kanban-plugin](https://github.com/DresvyanskiyDenis/claude-kanban-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/kanban-claude/agents/review-helper.md))
- **review-helper**: Helper agent for the creview skills (start / triage / respond / resolve / rounds), responsible for aggregation, compilation, analysis, and format & build verification. Assists the destination project's specialist agents and sticks to mechanical, procedural, template-driven work. ([OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/creview/agents/review-helper.md))
- **review-helper**: creview スキル群（start / triage / respond / resolve / rounds）の助手エージェント。集約・編纂・解析・フォーマット&ビルド検証を担う。移譲先プロジェクトの専門家エージェントを補佐し、機械的・手続的・テンプレート駆動の作業に徹する。 ([OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/OPENSPHERE-Inc/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/src/creview/agents/review-helper.md))
- **review-high**: Ревьювер правки (задача доски, MR или дифф ветки), когда agentctl pick --role review выдал effort high (неопределённость 3: в деталях есть развилки; сюда же неполные метаданные строки и любой вердикт уровня base, ему маппинг ниже high не выдаёт). Модель диспетчер передаёт параметром по первой строке вердикта. ([dronrider/devkit](https://github.com/dronrider/devkit/blob/HEAD/kit/agents/review-high.md))
- **review-history**: Check git blame and historical context of modified code ([prodelt/elt-harness](https://github.com/prodelt/elt-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/review-history.md) · ★1)
- **review-history**: /sdd:review のエージェント B。チームが過去の PR で受けた指摘の傾向 (指摘データ) を Phase の差分に当て、同じ指摘が付きそうな箇所を先に示す。読み取りだけで code は編集しない。 ([DaichiHoshina/spec-ramo](https://github.com/DaichiHoshina/spec-ramo/blob/HEAD/agents/review-history.md))
- **review-honesty-gates**: The resume validator can only catch what the truth document contradicts. Is the honesty invariant real or nominal? Read-only. ([akin-oz/harrier](https://github.com/akin-oz/harrier/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/review-honesty-gates.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-hunter**: Hunts one slice of what a round of the kit's code review changed, for defects the fixes brought into the code. Spawned fresh by the branch-review skill after each round of fixes, never picked on its own. ([ecstra/tensorlings](https://github.com/ecstra/tensorlings/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-hunter.md) · ★2)
- **review-hygiene**: Reviews code hygiene — dead code, unused exports, commented-out blocks, debug artifacts, stale TODOs, copy-paste duplication ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/blob/HEAD/agents/review-hygiene.md))
- **review-i18n**: Ревьюер переводов в незакоммиченных изменениях tanuki — имена ключей, качество ru/en, переиспользование ключей, plural-формы, типографика. Вызывается из /review; механику словарей уже проверил pnpm lint:i18n. ([Gabageba/tanuki](https://github.com/Gabageba/tanuki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-i18n.md))
- **review-i18n-copy**: Expert-review panel member. Reviews translation keys, namespaces, plurals, interpolation, accessibility strings, pseudo-locale and the i18n sync tooling against docs/i18n.md and the voice ADRs. Dispatched by the expert-review skill; read-only. ([rollercoaster-dev/Rollercoaster.dev-mobile](https://github.com/rollercoaster-dev/Rollercoaster.dev-mobile/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-i18n-copy.md))
- **review-idea-pack**: Fresh-context reviewer that gates docs/idea/idea-pack.md before the human approves it. Route here when the Idea Pack has been drafted (by the idea-pack skill or by hand) and someone asks to review, check, sanity-check, or sign off the Idea Pack, or before moving on to the PRD. Do NOT route here to write or fix the Idea Pack; this agent only judges. ([jiffi-co/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jiffi-co/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/builder-kit/agents/review-idea-pack.md))
- **review-images**: 只读审图子智能体：看一组资产图或分镜图，对照设定与提示词，回一份文字审图报告。generate-assets、generate-storyboard 的审核检查点由主 Agent 派发；只看图、读项目文件，不触发生成，不改动任何内容。 ([ArcReel/ArcReel](https://github.com/ArcReel/ArcReel/blob/HEAD/agent_runtime_profile/.claude/agents/review-images.md) · ★6.6k)
- **review-impact**: Reviews code changes for dropped guards and removed invariants (deleted lines) and for caller-side breakage from changed function signatures. ([synesenom/ran](https://github.com/synesenom/ran/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-impact.md) · ★15)
- **review-impact**: Invoked during the orc build and walkthru review steps (and util bump). Checks for breaking changes to public interfaces, unexpected side effects, performance regressions, and database query changes. ([joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins](https://github.com/joshevensen/hexbyte-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/orc/agents/review-impact.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **review-impact-completeness**: When a shared pattern is modified, find ALL consumers — including ad-hoc reimplementations that don't use shared code. The pattern behind the most subtle post-merge bugs. Catches: bypass paths that miss new capabilities, duplicate logic that diverged, code paths that should have changed but didn't. ([spinje/pflow](https://github.com/spinje/pflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-impact-completeness.md) · ★17)
- **review-impact-evaluator**: Scores the IMPACT of acting on a PR review — whether the agent's follow-up resolved the flagged issues and left the build green. Adjudicates resolution semantically (so multi-file and non-contiguous refactors count), uses match.py only as a cross-check, and hands all math to score.py. Run after review-quality-evaluator for the same run. Writes to evals/ only through score.py. ([Haadhi76/workflow](https://github.com/Haadhi76/workflow/blob/HEAD/Workflow_Evals/pr-review-evals/pr-review-evals/.claude/agents/review-impact-evaluator.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-impl**: Conformance gate. Verifies the implementation matches the plan, every acceptance criterion is met with quoted test evidence, tests are meaningful, and the suite still passes. Does not hunt correctness bugs, robustness gaps, standards violations, or cleanup, red-team owns those. Use after implementation chunks are complete, before final commit. ([KashZod/devloop](https://github.com/KashZod/devloop/blob/HEAD/agents/review-impl.md) · ★3)
- **review-implementation**: Reviews code changes after implementation for correctness, patterns compliance, and test coverage. Acts as a quality gate — returns PASS or REJECTED with specific issues. ([AbsolutSystems/absolutpowers](https://github.com/AbsolutSystems/absolutpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/review-implementation.md) · ★1)
- **review-implementer**: Implements a PR’s review action list, commits in small logical steps, and resolves GitHub review threads with “Done” replies when finished. Use when review-actions.md exists for a PR. ([prisma/orm](https://github.com/prisma/orm/blob/HEAD/skills-contrib/review-implement-phase/agents/review-implementer.md) · ★48k · 4 copies)
- **review-inbox-poller**: Cheap triage agent for ax-review-inbox. Given a batch of PRs (with title, body, size, age, staleness, CI/review state and labels already computed by tools/review-inbox.sh), writes a one-sentence summary of what each PR does and picks a priority (high/medium/low) using a fixed rubric. Pure judgment over the data it's given — makes no gh/API calls, reads no files, never comments or otherwise touches GitHub. ([garrefa/ai-notes](https://github.com/garrefa/ai-notes/blob/HEAD/agents/review-inbox-poller.md) · ★2)
- **review-infra**: Infra and architecture lens of the feature workflow's review round (docs/ops/REVIEW.md) — blast radius, IAM, SCPs and deploy roles, recurring cost, deploy order, rollback, workflows, dev slots, Lambda wiring. Runs when docs/ops/REVIEW.md picks it (Infra triggers; Round 2 and later); not for general use. ([radatta/aperture](https://github.com/radatta/aperture/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-infra.md))
- **review-infra**: Reviews container, CI and server config changes — validation, exposed ports, secrets, privileges, limits, pinned versions, rollback — and records a review JSON. ([yolol312/goodboy](https://github.com/yolol312/goodboy/blob/HEAD/agents/review-infra.md))
- **review-ingest**: Fresh-context reviewer that gates the artifacts derived by the ingest skill before anyone relies on the auto-accepted facts. Route here after ingest has scanned an existing prototype or repo and written derived artifacts, decisions, and the Prototype Bible, and someone asks to review, sanity-check, or sign off the ingestion before moving on to architect. Do NOT route here to write or fix the derived artifacts; this agent only judges. ([jiffi-co/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jiffi-co/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/builder-kit/agents/review-ingest.md))
- **review-intake**: Process external review feedback — read ALL items first, verify claims against codebase, then implement in priority order. Use when receiving PR comments or code review. ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/review-intake.md) · ★93)
- **review-intake**: Cold per-claim premise judge for incoming review findings. Dispatched by the gateway before any implementer sees a judgment-grade review finding. Verifies each claim against the code it cites and returns confirmed / falsified / needs-evidence per claim with the evidence line, plus a coverage line the gateway bounces on mismatch. Text only — writes nothing; falsified claims stop at the gateway. ([RockyHong/super-bootstrap](https://github.com/RockyHong/super-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/plugins/super-bootstrap/agents/review-intake.md) · ★3)
- **review-intake-agent**: Use when PR comments, review feedback or issue-tracker items arrive and a subagent should read them all, check each claim against the code, then act in priority order. Runs the review-intake skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/review-intake-agent.md) · ★1)
- **review-intent**: Intent alignment review — assesses whether the code changes actually deliver what the PR description claims, and whether the description accurately represents the changes ([samjonester/dotfiles](https://github.com/samjonester/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/personal/pi/agents/review-intent.md) · ★5)
- **review-intent**: Independent read-only intent reviewer. Compares the diff against stated acceptance criteria and non-goals to find unmet requirements, violated non-goals, undeclared behavior, and missing required edge cases. Judges "is this what was asked", not "is this elegant". Use when an intent file or PR description exists. ([Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins](https://github.com/Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-review/agents/review-intent.md))
- **review-intent-gate**: dialog-harness PR レビューの Phase 2-a 意図ゲート判定ワーカー（安価）。claude-review.yml の OC が Haiku ティアで起動する。PR 本文が実質的な diff の意図・トレードオフを述べているかを diff 単位で判定し JSON を返す。意図不明な diff は後段で修正提案を停止する根拠になる。 ([samejima-ai/dialog-harness](https://github.com/samejima-ai/dialog-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-intent-gate.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **review-interface-conformance**: Review seat: Interface conformance. Asks 3 lane(s) of a change set — design-system-conformance, accessibility, frontend-behavior-and-payload. Convene during a Dream Studio review when the change set touches this seat's scope. ([SeayInsights/dream-studio](https://github.com/SeayInsights/dream-studio/blob/HEAD/canonical/agents/review-interface-conformance.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **review-internal**: Code review specialist for own-repo PRs — correctness, security (OWASP Top 10), and pattern adherence. ([Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack](https://github.com/Manolii-org/ai-starter-pack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-internal.md) · 3 copies)
- **review-invariant-ecs**: Audits a PR diff against ECS correctness invariants in full file context and returns a review fragment with file:line citations. Use from review-pr when a PR touches systems, components, or entity-lifecycle code. ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-invariant-ecs.md) · ★10)
- **review-invariant-render**: Audits a PR diff that touches engine/render/, engine/prefabs/irreden/render/, or shaders against render-pipeline invariants (CPU/GPU struct layout, binding points, dispatch grids, lighting stages, GPU lifetime) and returns a review fragment with file:line citations. Use from review-pr whenever a PR touches render code or shader source. ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-invariant-render.md) · ★10)
- **review-invariants**: Repo-specific review rules for `ziw-code-review` (the org skill's bug taxonomy is generic; these are the traps that have actually bitten this codebase). Load this alongside the skill's checklist when reviewing Trace Flow diffs or PRs. ([zaks-io/trace-flow](https://github.com/zaks-io/trace-flow/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/review-invariants.md))
- **review-irreversible-operations**: Review seat: Irreversible operations. Asks 2 lane(s) of a change set — gitops-and-rollout-safety, data-and-migration. Convene during a Dream Studio review when the change set touches this seat's scope. ([SeayInsights/dream-studio](https://github.com/SeayInsights/dream-studio/blob/HEAD/canonical/agents/review-irreversible-operations.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **review-judge**: Validate review findings against the actual codebase, assess quality, filter false positives, and produce the final consolidated review. ([samjonester/dotfiles](https://github.com/samjonester/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/personal/pi/agents/review-judge.md) · ★5)
- **review-judge**: Juge l'écran réel d'une feature contre sa source (exigences, parcours voulu, glossaire), élément par élément, sur les captures d'une exécution, et rend un verdict par identifiant. Lancé par le skill forge:feature-review, jamais par l'agent qui a codé. ([ikoro-labs/forge](https://github.com/ikoro-labs/forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/forge/agents/review-judge.md) · ★1)
- **review-judge**: This role is mandatory only for schema v2 strict and the legacy code-review pipeline. Standard uses one independent general review; unresolved/conflicting findings needing adjudication must upgrade to strict before dispatching this role. Light has no judge. All adjudication requirements below apply to strict/legacy. Mode B must inspect the actual current revision and each finding's disposition; pr ([Fize/shipmate](https://github.com/Fize/shipmate/blob/HEAD/workflow-orchestrator/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Synthesizes verified verdicts, deterministic-gate state and risk tier into a single gate decision (PASS / PASS_WITH_NOTES / HUMAN_REQUIRED / BLOCK / REJECT_INTAKE) plus a concise human report. Only verified findings may block. Use as the final step of the multi-review pipeline. ([Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins](https://github.com/Jiusi-pys/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/multi-review/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Settles a conflict between the Standards axis and the Spec axis of a code review, where one says the code must be a certain way and the other says that same thing is wrong. Invoked by groundwork:code-review only for real contradictions, never as a routine pass over every finding. ([mauriciovieira/groundwork](https://github.com/mauriciovieira/groundwork/blob/HEAD/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Independent reviewer-readonly-v1 second pass. Reads changed files fresh from the main-thread REVIEW PACKET v1, checks cross-cutting gaps and panel quality, and performs no execution or mutation. ([MKITConsulting/zensu-copilot](https://github.com/MKITConsulting/zensu-copilot/blob/HEAD/upstream/zensu-claude-code/agents/review-judge.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-judge**: Zensu review-judge: confined read-only specialist; follows the parent review packet and repository instructions. ([MKITConsulting/zensu-copilot](https://github.com/MKITConsulting/zensu-copilot/blob/HEAD/src/plugin/com.github.copilot/agents/review-judge.agent.md))
- **review-judge**: Impartial judge for the adversarial review — receives red-team findings, independently reproduces each one, and marks it CONFIRMED, PLAUSIBLE, or REJECTED with evidence, merging duplicates and assigning severity. Optimized for precision; never edits project files. Used by the adversarial-review skill after the red-team phase. ([rns4731/cardiology-lead-engine](https://github.com/rns4731/cardiology-lead-engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judge**: Fresh-context judge for ONE dimension of a project review. Invoked only by the project-review skill, which supplies the evidence directory and the criteria file. Never delegate to this agent for any other task. ([vdaluz/claude-skills](https://github.com/vdaluz/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/general-workflow/agents/review-judge.md))
- **review-judgment**: dialog-harness PR レビュー Council の判定エージェント。claude-review.yml の OC が Phase 3-c で起動する。3 ペルソナ出力 + final_weights + conflict_type を受け取り、weighted_score = Σ(weight×confidence) で recommended を決定し、output-format.md §4 の判定 JSON のみ返す。final_decision は常に null。 ([samejima-ai/dialog-harness](https://github.com/samejima-ai/dialog-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-judgment.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **review-judgment**: The one LLM reviewer in phase 6. Judges intent, cross-file logic, architectural fit, and whether a disclosed shortcut is real. Everything mechanical is already enforced by `bun run doctor`. ([Eduardo-Barreto/web-project-template](https://github.com/Eduardo-Barreto/web-project-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-judgment.md))
- **review-karpathy**: Andrej Karpathy의 LLM 코딩 원칙 기반 리뷰어. "모델이 확인 없이 잘못된 가정을 하고, 코드를 과도하게 복잡하게 만든다"는 관찰을 바탕으로 단순성·수술적 변경·목표 지향 실행을 중심으로 코드를 리뷰합니다. 코드 작성 직후 선제적으로 사용하십시오. ([juyohan/genie-plugin](https://github.com/juyohan/genie-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/genie/agents/review-karpathy.md) · ★1)
- **review-kimi**: Code reviewer (Kimi). Analyzes diffs for bugs, security issues, and maintainability concerns. ([third774/dotfiles](https://github.com/third774/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/review-kimi.md) · ★6)
- **review-l3**: /home/misund/src/coopnorge/ai-engineering/etc/opencode/agents/review-l3.md ([misund/dotfiles](https://github.com/misund/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai/.config/opencode/agents/review-l3.md))
- **review-l4**: /home/misund/src/coopnorge/ai-engineering/etc/opencode/agents/review-l4.md ([misund/dotfiles](https://github.com/misund/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai/.config/opencode/agents/review-l4.md))

- **review-lad-wrapper**: Review-LAD specialist agent for the BMAD Agent Development Automator. Composes the upstream `lad_mcp_server` MCP `code_review` tool (Shelpuk-AI-Technology-Consulting/lad_mcp_server, version_floor `bb47e9e`, OpenRouter-backed dual-reviewer parallelism per ADR-008) and returns a sensor-not-advisor envelope. - **review-lane**: Read-only reviewer for one assigned dimension of a change, for a later run's verdict pass (with a new-defect pass when asked), or for a cold read of the whole change with no knowledge of earlier runs. - **review-lane**: L3 specialist reviewer for /pr-review — one lane (db, api, boundaries, guards) switched on by what the PR changes.
